    var ssSessionId = '';
    if (ssGetURLParameter('utm_source') != null) {
        ssSessionId = ssGetURLParameter('utm_source');
    }
    var gclid = '';
    if (ssGetURLParameter('gclid') != null) {
        gclid = ssGetURLParameter('gclid');
    }
    if (ssSessionId.toLowerCase() == 'omg') {
        ssSetCookie(ssSessionId);
    } else if (ssSessionId.toLowerCase() != 'omg' && ssSessionId.toLowerCase() != '' || gclid != '') {
        if (checkCookie("omg")) eraseCookie("omg");
    }

    function readCookie(name) {
        var nameEQ = name + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
        }
        return null;
    }

    function eraseCookie(name) {
        document.cookie = name + "=" + name + ";expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT;domain=;path=/";
    }

    function checkCookie(name) {
        return readCookie(name) != null;
    }

    function ssGetURLParameter(name) {
        return decodeURIComponent((new RegExp('[?|&]' + name + '=' + '([^&;]+?)(&|#|;|$)').exec(location.search) || [,""])[1].replace(/\+/g, '%20')) || null;
    }

    function ssSetCookie(cValue) {
        document.cookie = cValue + "=" + cValue + ";expires=" + ssCalculateExpireDate(30) + ";domain=;path=/";
    }

    function ssCalculateExpireDate(days) {
        var d = new Date();
        d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        d.toUTCString();
        return d;
    }

     require(['jquery'], function($) {
   $(document).ready(function(){
		  
    $('#search').on('input', function() {
  
        let searchTerm = $(this).val();
        
        if(searchTerm.length < 2){
            $('#search_results').html('<p class="wr-tr">Please enter at least 3 characters to search.</p>');
            return;
        }
        if(searchTerm != ''){
            $('.search-suggetion').show();
            $('.page-products .columns').css('z-index', '0');
			$('.owl-carousel').css('z-index', '0');
			
        }else{
            $('.search-suggetion').hide();
			$('.page-products .columns').css('z-index', '1');
			$('.owl-carousel').css('z-index', '1');
        }
        
        $.ajax({
            url: 'https://www.ishopping.pk/search/search.php',
            method: 'GET',
            data: { search_pr: searchTerm },
            beforeSend: function() {
                $('#search_results').html('<p>Searching...</p>');
            },
            success: function(response) {
                $('#search_results').html(response);
                $('body').addClass('no-scroll');
            },
            error: function() {
                $('#search_results').html('<p>We didn’t find anything for <strong>' + searchTerm + '</strong>. Try another search or visit our <a href="https://www.ishopping.pk/electronics.html">Electronics</a>.</p>');
            }

        });
    });
    $('.search-close').on('click', function() {
        $('.search-suggetion').hide();
        $('body').removeClass('no-scroll');
    });
});
});

    ], function ($, customerData) {
        $( document ).ready(function() {
            customerData.reload(['cart'], true);
            customerData.get('cart').subscribe(function (cartInfo) {
                var totalItems = null;
                cartInfo['items'].forEach(function(item) {
                    totalItems += item.qty;
                });
                if(totalItems) {
                    $('.cart-icon').attr("data-counter", totalItems);
                }else{
                    $('.cart-icon').attr("data-counter", 0);
                    $('.cart-icon').removeAttr("data-counter");

(function () {

    const slider = document.getElementById('menuSlider');
    const prev   = document.querySelector('.prev');
    const next   = document.querySelector('.next');
    const menuItems = slider.querySelectorAll('.menu-item');

    let isAnimating = false;
    let sliderPaused = false;

    function getItemWidth() {
        const item = slider.querySelector('.menu-item');
        return item ? item.offsetWidth : 0;
    }

    /* ---------- SLIDER CONTROLS ---------- */
    
    const SCROLL_ITEMS = 5;

    function slideNext() {
    if (isAnimating || sliderPaused) return;
    isAnimating = true;

    const itemWidth = getItemWidth();
    const scrollWidth = itemWidth * SCROLL_ITEMS;

    slider.style.transition = 'transform 0.55s ease';
    slider.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollWidth}px)`;

    slider.addEventListener('transitionend', function handler() {
        slider.style.transition = 'none';
        slider.style.transform = 'none';

        for (let i = 0; i < SCROLL_ITEMS; i++) {
            slider.appendChild(slider.firstElementChild);
        }

        isAnimating = false;
        slider.removeEventListener('transitionend', handler);
    });
}

    function slidePrev() {
    if (isAnimating || sliderPaused) return;
    isAnimating = true;

    const itemWidth = getItemWidth();
    const scrollWidth = itemWidth * SCROLL_ITEMS;

    slider.style.transition = 'none';

    for (let i = 0; i < SCROLL_ITEMS; i++) {
        slider.insertBefore(
            slider.lastElementChild,
            slider.firstElementChild
        );
    }

    slider.style.transform = `translateX(-${scrollWidth}px)`;

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        slider.style.transition = 'transform 0.55s ease';
        slider.style.transform = 'none';
    });

    slider.addEventListener('transitionend', function handler() {
        isAnimating = false;
        slider.removeEventListener('transitionend', handler);
    });
}

next && next.addEventListener('click', slideNext);
prev && prev.addEventListener('click', slidePrev);

    /* ---------- MEGA MENU HOVER FIX ---------- */

    menuItems.forEach(item => {

        item.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            sliderPaused = true;

            // Kill transform & transition to avoid stacking context
            slider.style.transition = 'none';
            slider.style.transform  = 'none';
        });

        item.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
            sliderPaused = false;
        });

    });

})();

    require(['jquery'], function ($) {
        $(document).ready(function () {
            $('.ajax-link').on('click', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                const $section = $(this).closest('.closest');
                $section.find('.ajax-link').removeClass('active');
                $(this).addClass('active');
                var catId = $(this).data('cate-id');
                var tab = $(this).data('tab') || 0;

                $section.find('.data-fatch-load').css('display', 'flex');

                $.ajax({
                    url: 'https://www.ishopping.pk/cron/home_product_fatch.php',
                    method: 'GET',
                    data: { cat_id: catId, pslider: tab },
                    success: function (response) {
                        $section.find('.home-3-col').html(response);
                    },
                    error: function (xhr, status, error) {
                        $('#ajax-response').html('<span>Error: ' + error + '</span>');
                    },
                    complete: function () {
                        $('.data-fatch-load').css('display', 'none');
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    });

</script><script>
    function showTimeLeftToday() {
        const now = new Date();

        // Midnight of next day
        const midnight = new Date();
        midnight.setHours(24, 0, 0, 0);

        // Difference in seconds
        let diff = Math.floor((midnight - now) / 1000);

        let hours = Math.floor(diff / 3600);
        let minutes = Math.floor((diff % 3600) / 60);
        let seconds = diff % 60;

        hours = hours.toString().padStart(2, "0");
        minutes = minutes.toString().padStart(2, "0");
        seconds = seconds.toString().padStart(2, "0");

        document.querySelector(".timer").innerHTML = `⏱ ${hours}h:${minutes}m:${seconds}s`;
    }

    // Update every second
    setInterval(showTimeLeftToday, 1000);

    // Run immediately
    showTimeLeftToday();
</script><script>

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

        document.querySelectorAll(".home-three-section-ctnt").forEach(function (section) {

            var ul = section.querySelector(".home-3-col ul");

            if (!ul) return;

            var left = document.createElement("button");
            left.innerHTML = "&#10094;";
            left.className = "product-scroll-left";

            var right = document.createElement("button");
            right.innerHTML = "&#10095;";
            right.className = "product-scroll-right";

            section.appendChild(left);
            section.appendChild(right);

            left.onclick = function () {
                ul.scrollBy({
                    left: -350,
                    behavior: "smooth"
                });
            };

            right.onclick = function () {
                ul.scrollBy({
                    left: 350,
                    behavior: "smooth"
                });
            };

        });

    });

    require([
        'jquery',
        'Magento_Customer/js/customer-data'
    ], function ($, customerData) {
        $( document ).ready(function() {
            customerData.reload(['cart'], true);
            customerData.get('cart').subscribe(function (cartInfo) {
                var totalItems = null;
                cartInfo['items'].forEach(function(item) {
                    totalItems += item.qty;
                });
                if(totalItems) {
                    $('.cart-icon-mobile').attr("data-counter", totalItems);
                }
            }, this);
        });
        $('#mobile-cat-anchor').click( function() {
            $(this).addClass('active').siblings().removeClass('active');

            $(this).addClass('active').siblings().removeClass('active');

            $('.mobile-menu-wrapper').toggle();
            $('body').toggleClass("mobile-menu-wrapper-expanded");

            $("#mobile-cat-anchor").removeClass("active");
        });

            $('.mobile-menu-wrapper').hide();

        /* ================= STATE ================= */
        let stack = ["Menu"];

        /* ================= RENDER ================= */
        function renderMenu(level){
            const container = $("#menuContainer");
            container.empty();

            const data = stack.reduce((o,k)=>o[k], menuData);

            $.each(data, function(name, value){
                if(typeof value === "string"){
                    container.append(`<a href="${value}" class="menu-link">${name}</a>`);
                } else {
                    container.append(`<button class="menu-item" data-key="${name}">${name} <span style="float: right;font-size: 11px;">❯</span></button></button>`);
                }
            });

            $("#menuTitle").text(level);
            $("#backBtn").toggle(stack.length > 1);
        }

        /* ================= OPEN ================= */
        $("#mobile-cat-anchor").click(function(){
            $(".mobile-menu-wrapper").addClass("active");
            stack = ["Menu"];
            renderMenu("Menu");
        });

        /* ================= CLOSE ================= */
        // $("#closeMenu").click(function(){
        //     $(".mobile-menu-wrapper").removeClass("active");
        // });

        /* ================= CLICK ITEM ================= */
        $(document).on("click",".menu-item",function(){
            const key = $(this).data("key");
            stack.push(key);
            renderMenu(key);
        });

        /* ================= BACK ================= */
        $("#backBtn").click(function(){
            stack.pop();
            renderMenu(stack[stack.length - 1]);
        });
    });
</script><script type="text/javascript">
require(['jquery'], function ($) {
    $(document).ready(function () {
        var footerPlaceholder = document.getElementById('lazy-footer-placeholder');
        var footerTemplate = document.getElementById('lazy-footer-template');

        function injectFooter() {
            try {
                if (!footerPlaceholder || !footerTemplate) return;
                if (footerPlaceholder.getAttribute('data-injected') === '1') return;
                footerPlaceholder.innerHTML = footerTemplate.innerHTML;
                footerPlaceholder.setAttribute('data-injected', '1');
                footerPlaceholder.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');

                // Re-bind mobile accordion toggles (use namespaced event to avoid duplicates)
                if (window.innerWidth <= 768) {
                    $('.tt').off('click.lazyFooter').on('click.lazyFooter', function () {
                        $(this).closest('.item').find('.nav-list').first().slideToggle();
                    });
                }

                // Optional: you can trigger any other JS initialisation for footer here
            } catch (e) {
                console.error('Failed to inject lazy footer', e);
            }
        }

        if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
            var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries, obs) {
                entries.forEach(function (entry) {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        injectFooter();
                        obs.unobserve(entry.target);
                    }
                });
            }, { rootMargin: '300px 0px' }); // preload when footer is 300px away
            if (footerPlaceholder) observer.observe(footerPlaceholder);
        } else {
            // fallback
            $(window).on('load', injectFooter);
        }

        // Debug helper: call window.__injectLazyFooter() in console to force injection
        window.__injectLazyFooter = injectFooter;
    });
});
